Method: 集成 6 个端侧 ONNX Runtime 模型（度量单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉/面部嵌入、人脸检测、自定义纸币检测器），少样本个人物品标注与检索管线，多模态反馈（罗马尼亚语语音合成、语音指令、振动、AR、空间音频、距离比例触觉），可选云端大模型（Gemini Flash）用于场景描述和自动标签。

Result: INT8 量化将深度推理延迟从约 1200ms 降至约 491ms；自定义纸币检测器 mAP@50 达 0.986；度量深度在 3m 内误差小于 1cm。

Conclusion: VisionAId 证明了仅用一部商品化智能手机即可实现实时、低延迟、多功能的视觉辅助系统，无需专用硬件，少样本个人物品定位是可落地的创新点。

Abstract: Over 285 million people worldwide live with a visual impairment, for whom everyday tasks such as avoiding obstacles, locating personal belongings, recognizing familiar faces, or handling cash remain persistent obstacles to personal autonomy. Existing assistive applications are typically limited to recognizing predefined categories, depend heavily on cloud connectivity, or require dedicated hardware. We present VisionAId, an Android application that turns a commodity smartphone into a real-time visual assistant. The system integrates six on-device deep learning models (metric monocular depth estimation, instance segmentation, visual and facial embeddings, face detection, and a custom banknote detector) running entirely through ONNX Runtime, with an optional cloud large language model (Google Gemini Flash) used only for narrative scene description and automatic object labeling. A distinctive contribution is a few-shot pipeline for personal objects: the user photographs an object from several angles, and the system later locates that specific instance in the environment, guiding the user toward it with augmented-reality markers, spatial audio, and distance-proportional haptics. All feedback is multimodal (Romanian speech synthesis, voice commands, vibration). On a reference device (Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra), INT8 quantization reduces depth latency from ~1200 ms to ~491 ms, the custom banknote detector reaches an mAP@50 of 0.986, and metric depth is calibrated to below 1 cm of error within 3 m.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Spectral and Polarimetric Clues for One-to-Multimodal Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02372)
*Federico Lincetto,Gianluca Agresti,Mattia Rossi,Piergiorgio Sartor,Pietro Zanuttigh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SPoILeR 方法，通过多模态预训练学习 RGB 与其他模态（红外、偏振、多光谱）的相关性，在微调阶段仅需 RGB 图像即可渲染出多视图一致的非传统模态图像，无需昂贵传感器采集新场景数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染方法扩展到多光谱、红外或偏振等成像模态时，依赖昂贵的传感器和标定设置来采集每个新场景的多模态数据，成本高、适用性受限。

Method: 采用多模态预训练阶段学习不同模态间的互相关性，然后仅用 RGB 图像监督的微调阶段预测非传统模态的渲染结果，实现多视图一致性。

Result: 在未提供任何红外、偏振或多光谱传感器输入样本的场景中，仍能准确渲染出这些模态的帧。

Conclusion: 证明了通过 RGB 图像结合跨模态预训练即可泛化到未见场景的非传统模态渲染，降低了多模态神经渲染的传感器成本。

Abstract: Neural rendering techniques allow for accurate reconstruction of the geometry and color appearance of 3D scenes. Some methods have extended their use to additional imaging modalities, such as multispectral, infrared, or polarimetric data. However, all of these approaches require expensive sensors and calibrated setups to capture new multimodal frames for each new scene. We propose Spectral and Polarimetric Implicit Learned Representation (SPoILeR), a novel method to obtain multi-view consistent renderings of unconventional modalities for scenes where either only RGB frames or very few of the additional modalities are available. Thanks to a multimodal pre-training phase, the model learns the mutual correlation between different modalities. This step allows predicting accurate renderings of unconventional modalities during a fine-tuning phase supervised only by RGB images. Experimental results show that the approach can accurately render infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral frames for scenes where no input sample captured by these types of sensors is provided.

</details>


### [110] [Representation Distribution Matching for One-Step Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02375)
*Lan Feng,Wuyang Li,Eloi Zablocki,Matthieu Cord,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iRDM提出通过匹配冻结预训练编码器下的生成图像与参考图像的特征分布来训练一步生成器，发现合理估计MMD、大batch size（>2048）和多编码器平衡匹配是关键，在ImageNet和FLUX.2上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索和优化Representation Distribution Matching (RDM)范式，解决经典MMD在生成任务中失效、单编码器易被欺骗、batch size影响等问题，训练出高质量的一步生成器。

Method: 系统研究RDM的两个设计轴（分布比较方式与表示空间），发现：1) 正确估计的MMD是强且可扩展的目标；2) 生成batch size最优值>2048；3) 单一表示可被欺骗，需用14个编码器的平衡电池匹配，并用SW_r14（Sliced-Wasserstein距离）评估。

Result: iRDM在ImageNet上SW_r14达1.30（一步生成SOTA），PickScore优于此前最佳一步生成器71.2%；将FLUX.2四步模型后训练为一步模型，GenEval 0.826 vs 0.794，PickScore 22.76 vs 22.58，仅用90 H200 GPU-hours。

Conclusion: 通过系统设计空间分析，iRDM实现了一步生成在质量和效率上的显著突破，且可推广到现有扩散模型的蒸馏加速中。

Abstract: We elucidate the design space of Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), our name for the paradigm that trains a one-step image generator by matching generated and reference feature distributions under frozen pretrained encoders. We identify two design axes, how the distributions are compared and the representations they are compared in, and controlled studies along them yield three findings. First, the classical MMD, which could not train convincing generators a decade ago, becomes a strong and scalable objective once estimated right. Second, the generated batch is then the operative variable, with an optimum above 2048, far beyond customary batch sizes. Third, any single representation can be gamed, driven below the real score while images stay visibly fake, so we match against a balanced battery of encoders and evaluate with SW_r14, a Sliced-Wasserstein distance over 14 encoders that is independent of the training loss and resists gaming. Combining the preferred choices yields improved RDM (iRDM): it sets the one-step state of the art on ImageNet at SW_r14 1.30, corroborated by PickScore, a human-preference proxy our objective never optimizes, which prefers it over the prior best one-step generator on 71.2% of matched samples. The same recipe post-trains the four-step FLUX.2 [klein] into a one-step generator, surpassing the four-step version on GenEval, 0.826 to 0.794, and on PickScore, 22.76 to 22.58, in 90 H200 GPU-hours. Project page: https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/.

</details>


### [111] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-II: Representational Similarity Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02386)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TGO-II框架分析ViT表示几何演化，发现训练中表示逐渐专业化、内在维度持续增加、token交互结构保持强耦合，表明表示复杂度与层专业化同时出现，流形扩展不依赖token解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注注意力机制和下游性能，对Vision Transformer内部表示的几何演化过程缺乏系统理解。

Method: 使用CKA、SVCCA、TwoNN-ID和token协方差分析四种指标，在ViT-Small/16上系统分析表示几何随训练的变化。

Result: CKA和SVCCA随训练逐渐下降（层专业化增强）；内在维度先增后稳（流形扩展）；token协方差和耦合分析表明强token交互结构始终存在。

Conclusion: 表示复杂度和层专业化同时出现，流形扩展不依赖token解耦，提出ViT通过逐步丰富的变换（而非token独立化）增加表示复杂性的新假说。

Abstract: While Vision Transformers have achieved remarkable success across computer vision and language applications, the geometric evolution of their internal representations throughout training remains insufficiently understood. Existing analyses primarily focus on attention mechanisms and downstream performance, leaving the evolution of representation geometry largely unexplored. In this work, we present Transformer Geometry Observatory-II (TGO-II), a representation geometry analysis framework designed to investigate how Transformer representations evolve during supervised training. TGO-II analyzes Vision Transformer (ViT-Small/16) representations using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), Singular Vector Canonical Correlation Analysis (SVCCA), Two-Nearest Neighbor Intrinsic Dimensionality (TwoNN-ID), and token covariance analysis. Our experiments reveal three key observations. First, both CKA and SVCCA progressively decrease throughout training, indicating increasing representational specialization across Transformer layers. Second, intrinsic dimensionality consistently increases before stabilizing, suggesting progressive expansion of the representation manifold into a larger set of locally accessible degrees of freedom. Third, token covariance and coupling analyses demonstrate that strong token interaction structure persists throughout training, challenging the hypothesis that increasing representational complexity arises primarily from progressive token independence. These findings suggest that representation complexity and layer specialization emerge simultaneously during training. Manifold expansion appears to occur without token decoupling. Together, these observations motivate a new hypothesis in which Vision Transformers increase representational complexity through progressively richer transformations while preserving strong token interaction structure during learning.

</details>


### [112] [Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02402)
*Nick Stracke,Kolja Bauer,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Miguel Angel Bautista,Josh Susskind,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VICIS任务：模型需从少量图像中推断共享视觉概念并迁移至新查询图像。现有VLM表现差，作者提出训练框架与架构，从图像集合中学习概念并提取概念特定嵌入，在合成数据和ImageNet/WordNet上展示更好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM能理解复杂文本指令，但难以从纯视觉上下文中推理共享概念，即无法从示例图像集合中推断概念并应用到新输入上。

Method: 提出VICIS任务基准 + 一个训练框架与架构，学习从图像集合中推断视觉概念并从查询中提取概念特定嵌入，实现概念保持的图像生成。

Result: SOTA VLM在该任务上表现差，常忽略视觉上下文或生成有偏结果。作者方法在合成数据和ImageNet/WordNet上生成更准确、更多样的输出，并泛化到未见概念和草图模态。

Conclusion: 当前VLM缺乏从图像集合中推断视觉概念的能力，所提方法有效填补这一空白，并展示出向未见概念和跨模态泛化的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.

</details>


### [113] [Object-centric LeJEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02404)
*Jakob Geusen,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于对象级对齐的LeJEPA方法，利用SAM预生成掩码替代不稳定的自监督场景分割，在COCO上训练后，在跟踪、分类、分割、重识别任务上超越图像级LeJEPA基线，数据效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像级自监督方法（如LeJEPA）需要大量训练数据，而对象级对齐理论上更数据高效，但完全自监督的联合分割与表征学习存在循环依赖不稳定问题。

Method: 在训练期间使用SAM提供的现成对象掩码，将LeJEPA的分布抗坍塌目标从整图扩展到可变大小的对象集合，同时引入实例分离损失（同场景其他对象作为负样本）。

Result: 在两种模型规模和10-100% COCO数据上，对象级LeJEPA在DAVIS跟踪、ImageNet-1k分类、ADE20k分割、NAVI重识别上均优于图像级LeJEPA。

Conclusion: 利用现成掩码就可以稳定实现对象级自监督对齐，提升数据效率与下游性能，无需复杂的自监督分割循环。

Abstract: Image encoders trained with LeJEPA can deliver strong features for downstream tasks, but, like other image-level self-supervised methods, typically require large training datasets. Aligning representations at the level of objects rather than whole scenes promises greater data efficiency, but doing this in a completely self-supervised way, effectively jointly partitioning a scene and representing its objects, is unstable: the two are locked in a cyclic dependency, partitioning requires meaningful representations, while meaningful representations require consistent partitioning. We sidestep this instability by taking object masks as given during training, using cheap, off-the-shelf SAM proposals. We extend LeJEPA - whose distributional anti-collapse objective ports naturally from whole images to variable-sized sets of objects - to align object-centric representations rather than whole images. An additional instance-separating loss, which treats other objects in the same scene as negatives, further boosts downstream performance. Across two model scales and 10-100% of COCO, object-level LeJEPA outperforms image-level LeJEPA on tracking (DAVIS), classification (ImageNet-1k), segmentation (ADE20k), and re-identification (NAVI).

</details>


### [114] [Wavelet-Guided Semantic Signal Compensation for Inversion-Free Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02421)
*Anqi Tang,Wenhao Sun,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无反转、频率感知的语义补偿策略，用于文本引导图像编辑。该方法在早期生成阶段增强有效信号，提升全局编辑能力同时保持背景结构一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无反转图像编辑框架（如FlowEdit）在全局属性偏移时，高噪声区间的流倾向于主导流向数据流形，削弱文本条件方向的影响，导致全局编辑不足或背景结构保留不理想。

Method: 提出频率感知的语义补偿策略：在生成早期阶段（高噪声区域）增强文本条件信号的有效强度，同时维持背景结构的低频一致性，无需反转过程。

Result: 在全局属性偏移场景下，该方法提升了全局编辑能力，同时不牺牲背景保真度（具体定量/定性结果需看完整论文）。

Conclusion: 频率感知的语义补偿策略有效解决了无反转编辑框架在全局属性修改时的局限性，实现了更好的编辑质量-背景保留权衡。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing aims to modify visual content according to a target prompt while preserving the background. Recent inversion-free image editing frameworks such as FlowEdit have demonstrated strong editing capability without requiring inversion. Empirically, FlowEdit can achieve substantial semantic changes under appropriate hyperparameter settings. However, we observe that under certain global attribute shifts, the editing trajectory may not effectively move away from the source distribution in the early timesteps. Our analysis suggests that in the high-noise regime, the dominant manifold-seeking flow toward the data manifold can reduce the influence of the text-conditioned direction, leading to limited global modification while background structures remain only moderately preserved. Inspired by this observation, we propose an inversion-free, frequency-aware semantic compensation strategy that strengthens the effective signal in the early stage of generation, while maintaining structural consistency in the background. The proposed method improves global editing capacity without sacrificing background fidelity.

</details>


### [115] [Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02425)
*Francesca Pistilli,Simone Alberto Peirone,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SG-Ego数据集（基于Ego4D扩展的时空场景图）和GLEN模型（图序列推理），以及A-GEF任务（活动驱动的图编辑预测），用于结构化理解第一人称视频中的人-环境交互动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖隐式视觉或语言对齐表示，缺乏对场景动态的结构化推理。显式、可组合、可编辑的人-环境交互表示对于丰富的接地活动理解至关重要。

Method: 1) SG-Ego: 在Ego4D上标注时空场景图，将关系三元组随时间聚合为显式的场景状态演化描述。2) GLEN: 基于图的模型，对场景图序列进行文本动作对齐和时间演化建模。3) A-GEF: 将场景动态建模为受持续活动驱动的结构化变换序列的新型预测任务。

Result: 在EgoMCQ、EgoCVR、EXPLORE-Bench和A-GEF基准上，GLEN优于原始视频基线，在推理场景中表现优异，可与MLLM匹敌，同时支持可控和结构化的场景动态预测。

Conclusion: 时空场景图及其推理模型可作为视频理解（及更广领域）的强组合性和可解释性表示。

Abstract: Understanding human behavior while interacting with the surrounding world is crucial for many applications of embodied AI. First-person videos are particularly informative for this problem, as they well capture how activities reshape the scene over time. However, existing approaches often rely on implicit visual or language-aligned representations, disregarding structured reasoning over the scene dynamic. We argue that explicit, compositional and editable representations of human-environment interactions can play a crucial role for rich grounded activity understanding. To this end, we introduce SG-Ego, a large scale annotation set extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs, where relations triplets are consolidated over time into explicit time-evolving descriptions of the scene state. To reason over this representation, we propose GLEN, a graph-based model that operates over scene graph sequences to both align them with textual actions and model their temporal evolution. In addition, we formulate the activity-driven graph-edit forecasting (A-GEF) problem, a novel task that casts scene dynamics as a sequence of structured transformations conditioned on ongoing actions, enabling explicit reasoning about how scenes change over time. We validate our approach across multiple downstream tasks, spanning retrieval benchmarks as EgoMCQ and EgoCVR, as well as long-horizon reasoning benchmarks as EXPLORE-Bench and the newly introduced A-GEF. GLEN achieves strong results compared to raw video baselines and it excels in reasoning settings, typically addressed only with MLLMs, while enabling controllable and structured predictions of scene dynamics driven by human activities. We believe our results establish spatio-temporal scene graphs, together with models that reason over them, as strong compositional and interpretable representations for video understanding and potentially beyond.

</details>


### [116] [MARVEL: Margin-Aware Robust von Mises-Fischer Expert Learning for Long-Tailed Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02435)
*A. S. Anudeep,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MARVEL框架，通过非线性von Mises-Fisher分类器、多专家机制和异常检测专家，解决医学图像中类别不平衡和域外检测问题，在三个医学数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法大多假设平衡数据集、使用粗粒度或非临床OOD源评估，缺乏对临床多样化OOD场景的全面评估，且医学数据天然存在严重类别不平衡问题。

Method: 三个核心组件：(1) 非线性von Mises-Fisher (NvMF)分类器，理论证明其与余弦分类器的渐近联系；(2) 多专家框架，每个NvMF分类器专注标签分布的不同区域以处理不平衡；(3) 异常检测专家，显式区分内点与离群点数据。

Result: 在RFMiD、ISIC2019、NCTCRC上FPR95分别降低8.45%、13.02%、36.90%，一致超越SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能可靠识别陌生病例并转诊给临床医生，支持更安全的AI辅助诊断。

Abstract: For clinical deployment, it is essential that automated diagnostic systems remain reliable when confronted with previously unseen cases, yet deep models routinely misclassify out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs with high confidence, underscoring the need for more robust OOD detection methods. Although substantial effort has been devoted to improving model robustness, most of the existing literature assumes balanced datasets, evaluates OOD detection on coarse or non-clinical OOD sources, or lacks comprehensive assessment across diverse OOD scenarios. To address the gaps, we propose a novel methodology trained on diverse and imbalanced medical datasets and evaluated across a clinically reflective OOD spectrum. Our framework comprises three key components: (1) a Nonlinear von Mises-Fisher (NvMF) classifier capable of learning non-linear decision boundaries, with theoretical proof of its asymptotic connection to cosine classifiers; (2) a multi-expert framework in which margin-aware NvMF classifiers specialise in different regions of label distribution to better handle imbalance; and (3) an outlier expert trained explicitly to distinguish inlier from outlier data, thereby strengthening OOD detection. Evaluation on RFMiD, ISIC2019, and NCTCRC datasets demonstrates consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving mean FPR95 reductions of 8.45%, 13.02%, and 36.90% respectively. These gains are further supported by comprehensive ablations that validated the contributions of each component. This enables reliable identification of unfamiliar cases for deferral to clinicians, supporting safer AI-assisted diagnosis in real-world workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/redboxup/MARVEL.

</details>


### [117] [OrbitQuant: Data-Agnostic Quantization for Image and Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02461)
*Donghyun Lee,Jitesh Chavan,Duy Nguyen,Sam Huang,Liming Jiang,Priyadarshini Panda,Timo Mertens,Saurabh Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrbitQuant是一种数据无关的权值-激活量化方法，通过在归一化旋转基（随机置换块Hadamard变换）中量化扩散Transformer，消除对校准数据的依赖。单个Lloyd-Max码本即可覆盖所有时间步、提示和层，并支持W2A4低比特量化，在FLUX.1、Wan 2.1等多个DiT模型上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）推理成本高，现有后训练量化（PTQ）方法需要为每个新checkpoint或模态重新拟合校准数据，因为DiT激活值随时间步、提示和引导分支剧烈变化。作者希望设计一种无需校准数据、通用跨模态的量化方法。

Method: 提出OrbitQuant，核心思路是在归一化旋转基（随机置换块Hadamard变换，RPBH）中进行量化。该旋转将每个坐标集中到固定的已知边缘分布上，从而单个Lloyd-Max码本即可覆盖所有时间步、层和输入。权重量化通过将旋转吸收到权重内部（离线完成），运行时只需对激活做前向旋转。方法从图像到视频无需任何模态相关调参。

Result: 在FLUX.1、Z-Image-Turbo、Wan 2.1、CogVideoX等多个DiT模型上，在多个低比特设置下达到PTQ SOTA。将图像扩散Transformer的PTQ推进到W2A4并保持可用生成质量。

Conclusion: OrbitQuant是一种数据无关、跨模态通用的DiT量化方法，显著降低了PTQ的部署成本，同时保持甚至提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image and video generation, but their multi-step sampling and growing parameter count make inference expensive. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is the natural remedy, yet DiT activations shift across timesteps, prompts, and guidance branches, forcing prior methods to re-fit calibration data for every new checkpoint or modality. We present OrbitQuant, a data-agnostic weight-activation quantizer that bypasses range estimation by quantizing in a normalized, rotated basis. In this basis, a randomized permuted block-Hadamard (RPBH) rotation concentrates each coordinate around one fixed, known marginal regardless of the input, so a single Lloyd-Max codebook serves all timesteps, prompts, and layers of a given input dimension. We extend the same quantizer to weight rows offline, absorbing the rotation into the weights so that it cancels inside each linear layer and only a forward rotation on the activations remains at runtime. The same recipe transfers from image to video with no per-modality tuning. Across FLUX.1, Z-Image-Turbo, Wan 2.1, and CogVideoX, it sets the state of the art for PTQ at several low-bit settings. It also pushes PTQ of image diffusion transformers to W2A4 with usable generation quality.

</details>


### [118] [Interpretation-Oriented Cloud Removal via Observation-Anchored Residual Flow with Geo-Contextual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02471)
*Ziyao Wang,Maonan Wang,Yucheng He,Xianping Ma,Ziyi Wang,Hongyang Zhang,Yirong Cheng,Man-on Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR)，一种联合保证云移除中图像重建保真度和后续下游任务可解释性的统一框架。核心创新是Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow)将云移除建模为物理有依据的残差反演过程，以及Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA)利用视觉基础模型约束语义流形。在六个CR数据集和十二个下游任务上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云移除方法过度追求视觉真实感，忽视了其对下游任务（如语义分割、变化检测）的影响，导致语义漂移和下游性能下降。

Method: 1) OAR-Flow：将生成轨迹锚定在有云观测而非纯噪声上，将CR重新建模为物理有依据的残差反演过程，实现快速稳定重建。2) GCPA：利用视觉基础模型（VFM）的语义先验约束重建在语义流形内，保持空间-语义完整性。

Result: 在六个CR数据集和十二个下游任务上，GACR在重建质量上优于现有方法，并持续提升下游任务准确率。

Conclusion: GACR有效弥合了云移除与下游解释之间的差距，实现了忠实重建与鲁棒可解释性的统一。

Abstract: Cloud removal (CR) is essential for optical remote sensing, serving as a prerequisite for reliable downstream interpretation, such as semantic segmentation and change detection. However, existing CR approaches often prioritize visual realism while overlooking their impact on subsequent analytical tasks, leading to semantic drift and degraded downstream performance. To address this issue, we propose Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR), a unified framework that jointly ensures faithful reconstruction and robust interpretability. At its core, GACR incorporates Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow), which reformulates CR as a physically grounded residual inversion process. By anchoring the generative trajectory to the cloudy observation rather than pure noise, OAR-Flow enables fast, stable, and faithful reconstruction. To further preserve semantic structures critical for downstream interpretation, GACR integrates Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA) to constrain the reconstruction within a semantic manifold induced by a Vision Foundation Model (VFM). Consequently, GACR strictly maintains the spatial-semantic integrity of complex landscapes. Extensive experiments across six CR datasets and twelve downstream tasks demonstrate that GACR produces superior reconstruction quality while consistently improving downstream task accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/wzy6055/GACR.

</details>


### [119] [EAGLE-360: Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration in 360$^\circ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02479)
*Jingtao Xu,Zizhuo Lin,Jianwen Sun,Yi Yang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EAGLE-360 提出了一种全局到局部的主动探索框架，结合RoPE Rolling编码和GRPO训练，在360°全景视觉搜索任务中实现了近8倍的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLLM无法有效建模全景图像的极地畸变和连续圆柱拓扑结构，导致目标检测精度低；现有方法依赖碎片化局部视角，缺乏全局先验，搜索效率低下且难以从视野丢失中恢复。

Method: 提出EAGLE-360框架：1) 全局先验建立初始全景视角，迭代推理逐步缩小搜索空间；2) 引入RoPE Rolling坐标偏移位置编码建模全景连续拓扑；3) 构建包含14000+张4K全景图和70000+轮VQA对话的大规模数据集；4) 采用SFT+GRPO训练流程提升空间推理和工具调用能力。

Result: 在360°视觉搜索任务上达到新SOTA，准确率相比基线模型提升近8倍，探索效率显著增强。

Conclusion: 全局到局部的主动探索范式结合适合全景的位置编码和强化学习训练，能够有效克服全景搜索中的根本性挑战。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in standard visual understanding, adapting them for active visual search in 360$^\circ$ panoramic environments exposes fundamental limitations. Specifically, standard MLLMs struggle to effectively model inherent panoramic properties, such as severe polar distortion and continuous cylindrical topologies, which significantly degrades target detection accuracy. Consequently, existing panoramic search methods attempt to compensate by relying heavily on fragmented local viewpoints. Burdened by rigid initialization and a lack of global panoramic priors, these approaches suffer from myopic, inefficient exploration and struggle with robust error recovery when targets are out of view. To overcome these challenges, we propose EAGLE-360, a novel Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration framework. Rather than performing exhaustive local searches, EAGLE-360 leverages global priors to establish an initial holistic perspective, iteratively reasoning and progressively narrowing the search space. Architecturally, we adapt RoPE Rolling, a coordinate-shifting positional encoding mechanism, to seamlessly model the continuous topologies of panoramas. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct the large-scale EAGLE-360 dataset, comprising 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ rounds of high-quality VQA dialogues. By employing a training pipeline that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), we effectively elicit complex spatial reasoning and tool-calling capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EAGLE-360 establishes a new state-of-the-art for 360$^\circ$ visual search, achieving nearly an 8-fold increase in accuracy over the base model while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [Combating Textual Noise and Redundancy: Entropy-Aware Dense Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02484)
*Xuehui Wang,Xuankun Yang,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉语言模型在密集指令和细粒度查询下视觉token剪枝失效的问题，提出基于熵感知的密集剪枝框架EADP，通过统计熵过滤文本噪声得到鲁棒的细粒度指令相关性分数，并将token选择建模为带空间先验的子模最大化问题，实现结构化压缩，在严格token预算下保持细粒度视觉线索，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在密集指令和细粒度查询场景下无法保留关键视觉线索，原因是：(1) 文本噪声广泛分散，污染密集跨模态评分；(2) 标准token选择导致特征碎片化。

Method: (1) 利用统计熵量化并过滤文本噪声，生成鲁棒的细粒度指令相关性分数；(2) 将token选择建模为带空间先验的子模最大化问题，确保整体且非冗余的视觉表征。

Result: 在多项多模态基准上达到SOTA性能，在严格token预算下显著改善了VLMs的精度-效率权衡，稳健保留了细粒度视觉线索。

Conclusion: EADP通过熵感知去噪与子模优化两个核心设计，有效解决了密集指令场景下token剪枝的瓶颈问题，是一种高效且鲁棒的结构化压缩方案。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is a crucial strategy for accelerating VLMs by compressing redundant image patches, yet existing methods often fail to preserve critical cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries. In this paper, we investigate this failure and identify two underlying bottlenecks: the widespread dispersion of textual noise that corrupts dense cross-modal scoring, and the feature fragmentation inherent to standard token selection. To address these issues, we propose Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), a framework that reformulates pruning as a structured compression problem. EADP first leverages statistical entropy to quantify and filter out textual noise, yielding a robust, fine-grained instruction relevance score. Subsequently, instead of naive Top-K selection, EADP casts token selection as a submodular maximization problem with a spatial prior, explicitly ensuring a holistic and non-redundant visual representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, robustly preserving fine-grained visual cues under strict token budgets while achieving SoTA performance on challenging multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [121] [GeoMix: Descriptor-Free Visual Localization via Global Context and Multi-Detector Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02486)
*Yejun Zhang,Xinjue Wang,Zihan Wang,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMix 是一种无描述符的 2D-3D 匹配框架，通过局部几何增强、全局上下文聚合和多检测器混合训练，在视觉定位任务中将旋转误差降低89%、平移误差降低90%，大幅缩小了与描述符方法的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无描述符视觉定位精度远落后于描述符方法，原因是几何匹配的判别性不足：局部几何信息利用不充分、缺乏全局上下文、过度依赖单一关键点检测器。作者发现无描述符匹配天然支持多检测器联合训练，利用这一特性提升匹配判别力。

Method: 1) 局部：方向与距离感知嵌入增强邻域聚合的细粒度空间结构；2) 全局：可学习上下文节点通过交叉注意力聚集和分发场景级信息；3) 训练级：Mix-Training 利用检测器无关的几何空间，在多个关键点检测器上联合学习表示。

Result: 在 MegaDepth、Cambridge Landmarks、7Scenes、Aachen Day-Night 上达到无描述符方法 SOTA，75% 旋转误差降低 89%，平移误差降低 90%，零样本泛化到未见过的检测器，缩小了与描述符方法的差距。

Conclusion: 通过多层级增强几何判别性的策略有效弥补了无描述符定位的精度短板，且检测器无关的几何空间设计具备良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Descriptor-free visual localization eliminates high-dimensional descriptor storage, preserves scene privacy, and simplifies map maintenance, yet its accuracy still lags far behind descriptor-based pipelines. We identify this gap to insufficient geometric discriminability in geometry-only matching. Without visual appearance, current methods underutilize local geometry cues, lack the global context among keypoints, and overfit to a single keypoint detector. We further observe that descriptor-free matching naturally enables multi-detector training, as heterogeneous keypoints can be optimized in a shared geometry-only space without aligning descriptor spaces. Building on these insights, we propose GeoMix, a descriptor-free 2D-3D matching framework that strengthens geometric discriminability at three levels. Locally, directional and distance-aware embeddings enrich neighborhood aggregation with fine-grained spatial structure. Globally, learnable context nodes aggregate and redistribute scene-wide information via cross-attention to resolve ambiguities beyond local receptive fields. At the training level, Mix-Training exploits this detector-agnostic geometry space to learn representations across multiple keypoint detectors. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, Cambridge Landmarks, 7Scenes, and Aachen Day-Night show that GeoMix sets a new state of the art among descriptor-free methods, reducing 75th-percentile rotation error by 89\% and translation error by up to 90\% over the previous best, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen detectors and narrowing the gap to descriptor-based pipelines. Code is available at $\href{https://github.com/YejunZhang/Geomix}{\text{this links}}$.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02494)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIP模型存在文本出现在图像中时视觉表征被词汇语义误导的脆弱性（Typographic Attack）。本文提出无训练的可解释性方法，通过采样分析隐藏状态、归因注意力头、挖掘电路定位编码词汇信息的ViT组件，并通过对这些电路进行简单干预（如调整注意力权重）显著提升对打字攻击的鲁棒性，且在多个LVLM的VQA任务上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP作为LVLM的视觉编码器，对图像中无关文本（Typographic Attack）存在严重鲁棒性问题，视觉表征偏向词汇语义而非真实视觉语义，这对自动驾驶等安全关键应用构成风险，而现有方法缺乏可解释且有效的防御手段。

Method: 提出一种无训练的机械可解释性方法：(1) 对隐藏状态进行基于采样的解释；(2) 定量归因每个注意力头对语义vs词汇的关注；(3) 通过概率分析和电路挖掘，定位ViT中过度编码词汇信息的组件；(4) 对定位到的电路施加简单的无训练干预（如选择性调整注意力权重）。

Result: 该方法在目标分类任务上优于监督式和训练式防御方法；将干预应用于多个SOTA LVLM的视觉编码器后，在RIO-Bench上VQA准确率显著提升，证实了方法的有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过定位并干预编码词汇信息的特定ViT电路，无需训练即可有效抵御Typographic Attack，且方法具有泛化性，适用于多个LVLM架构。

Abstract: Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench. These results confirm both the efficacy and the generalizability of our mechanistic approach. Code is released at https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR.

</details>


### [123] [Seek to Segment: Active Perception for Panoramic Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02497)
*Song Tang,Shuming Hu,Xincheng Shuai,Henghui Ding,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出主动全景指代分割(APRS)新任务，让智能体在360°环境中通过主动旋转视角寻找用户指定物体并分割。提出PanoSeeker，利用VLM+EgoSphere空间记忆进行高效搜索轨迹规划，经SFT+RL训练，在基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代分割方法处理静态固定视角图像，无法应用于Embodied AI中需要在连续360°环境中主动感知的智能体，存在任务定义与方法空白。

Method: PanoSeeker: (1) VLM + EgoSphere显式空间视觉记忆，将局部观测逐步集成到统一360°表征中；(2) 通过记忆规划高效不冗余的搜索轨迹；(3) 找到目标后进行主动视角对齐并输出分割掩码；(4) 专家标注轨迹数据集SFT + RL训练优化搜索效率。

Result: 在新建立的APRS基准上，PanoSeeker在搜索效率和分割精度上显著优于适配的SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: 主动全景指代分割是连接指代分割与Embodied AI的重要新任务，PanoSeeker通过记忆增强的VLM框架有效解决了该问题。

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation models passively process static images captured from fixed perspectives, limiting their applicability in Embodied AI, where agents must perform active perception in the continuous 360$^\circ$ environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task: Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS). In this setting, an agent is required to adjust its viewing direction ($Δθ, Δφ$) to explore the 360$^\circ$ environment, seeking the object specified by a user instruction for segmentation. To tackle this challenging task, we propose PanoSeeker, a memory-augmented agent for efficient APRS. Rather than relying on heuristic scanning, PanoSeeker integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with EgoSphere, an explicit spatial visual memory. By progressively integrating sequential local observations into a unified 360$^\circ$ representation, EgoSphere enables the agent to plan efficient and non-redundant search trajectories. Once the target is found, the agent performs active viewpoint alignment and outputs the segmentation mask. Furthermore, we curate an expert-annotated search trajectory dataset with memory timelines for Supervised Fine-Tuning, followed by Reinforcement Learning post-training to explicitly optimize PanoSeeker's exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments on our newly established APRS benchmark demonstrate that PanoSeeker achieves superior search efficiency and segmentation accuracy, significantly outperforming adapted state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [124] [From SRA to Self-Flow: Data Augmentation or Self-Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02508)
*Dengyang Jiang,Mengmeng Wang,Harry Yang,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Self-Flow中双时间步调度（dual-time scheduling）的改进机制，通过引入Attention Separation方法隔离不同噪声水平token间的交互，发现Self-Flow相比SRA的提升主要来自数据增强而非跨噪声token交互。Attention Separation本身通过将单张图像拆分为多个有效训练部分来提供增强效果，结合自表示对齐、双时间步和注意力分离增强在ImageNet上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Self-Flow（双时间步调度）相比SRA性能提升的真正原因：究竟是因为不同噪声水平的token间交互（cleaner tokens帮助noisier tokens），还是因为沿噪声维度的数据增强。通过解耦这两个因素来厘清机制。

Method: 提出Attention Separation方法，在保持与Self-Flow相同的双时间步输入前提下，阻断不同噪声水平token之间的注意力交互。通过消融对比验证数据增强才是性能提升的关键因素。

Result: 移除不同噪声水平token间的交互（Attention Separation）不仅不降低性能，甚至有所提升，证明Self-Flow的改进主要来自数据增强；Attention Separation本身通过将单张图像拆分为多个有效训练部分提供了增强效果。

Conclusion: Self-Flow相比SRA的提升主要来自沿噪声维度的数据增强而非跨token交互；结合自表示对齐、双时间步和注意力分离增强的方法在ImageNet上有效。

Abstract: Representation alignment has become an effective way to accelerate diffusion transformer training and improve generation quality. Recent self-alignment methods, such as SRA and Self-Flow, further remove the dependency on external pretrained encoders by constructing alignment within the diffusion model itself. However, the mechanism behind the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow, dual-time scheduling, remains under-examined: Self-Flow attributes its gain to interactions between tokens at different noise levels, where cleaner tokens help infer noisier ones. In this work, we revisit this explanation and ask whether the gain instead comes from data augmentation along the noise dimension. To disentangle these factors, we introduce Attention Separation, which preserves the same dual-timestep input as Self-Flow while blocking attention between tokens assigned to different noise levels. Surprisingly, removing such interaction does not degrade performance and can even improve it, suggesting that the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow mainly comes from data augmentation. Furthermore,We show that Attention Separation itself provides an augmentation effect by splitting a single image into multiple effective training parts to expand the training data. Based on these observations, we combine self-representation alignment with dual-timestep and attention-separation augmentation, and demonstrate the effectiveness of this design on ImageNet.

</details>


### [125] [PointDiT: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Monocular Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02515)
*Haofei Xu,Rundi Wu,Philipp Henzler,Nikolai Kalischek,Michael Oechsle,Fabian Manhardt,Marc Pollefeys,Andreas Geiger,Federico Tombari,Michael Niemeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种极简的像素空间扩散Transformer（基于纯ViT），直接在原始3D点图块上操作，以DINOv3图像token为条件，从头训练扩散骨干网络，无需点图分词器，在单图3D重建上超越复杂的潜在扩散模型和混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图3D重建方法依赖复杂的混合架构和损失函数，或将几何压缩到潜在空间以利用预训练潜在扩散模型。本文证明这些架构开销和复杂损失是不必要的。

Method: 基于纯ViT的像素空间扩散Transformer，直接在原始3D点图块上操作，以预训练DINOv3的图像token为条件，从头训练扩散骨干，无需点图分词器。

Result: 在单图3D重建任务上超越复杂的潜在扩散模型和混合替代方案，生成更锐利的几何结构，在透明物体等高模糊区域更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 简单架构加上像素空间扩散即可达到甚至超越复杂方法，无需潜在压缩和复杂损失设计。

Abstract: State-of-the-art single-image 3D reconstruction methods often rely on complex hybrid architectures and loss functions, or compress geometry into latent spaces in order to leverage pre-trained latent diffusion models. In this work, we show that such architectural overhead and intricate loss formulations are unnecessary. We introduce a minimalist pixel-space Diffusion Transformer, built on a plain ViT, that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from a pre-trained DINOv3. Unlike existing latent diffusion approaches, we train our diffusion backbone entirely from scratch, eliminating the need for point map tokenizers. Despite its simplicity, our approach surpasses complex latent-based diffusion models while remaining significantly simpler than hybrid alternatives. Notably, it produces sharper geometric structure and is more robust in highly ambiguous regions, such as transparent objects.

</details>


### [126] [Alignment Is All You Need For X-to-4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02516)
*Qiaowei Miao,Kehan Li,Yawei Luo,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align4D 是一个将任意模态输入（如文本、图像、视频、3D）转化为一致4D内容（动态3D场景）的框架，通过视频引导运动、3D数据塑造几何，并引入距离对齐、联合约束和异步优化三个关键技术，在X4D和Consistent4D基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以实现任意用户定义模态到4D的生成，主要因为多模态数据集构建成本高、现有可扩展性有限，缺少统一的X-to-4D生成框架。

Method: (1) 物体距离对齐：搜索视频对齐和多视角对齐的物体距离，协调4D渲染与视频及多视角扩散先验；(2) 运动-几何联合对齐：通过同步视频与3D输入约束已知和未知视图，保证一致性；(3) 异步优化：解耦高斯属性与形变网络训练，提升运动与几何保真度。

Result: 在X4D和Consistent4D基准上，Align4D在X-to-4D生成中取得了质量与一致性的SOTA表现。

Conclusion: Align4D提供了一个灵活、可扩展的任意模态到4D生成框架，通过视频-3D协同引导实现了高质量、一致的动态3D内容生成。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models excel at synthesizing high-quality images, videos, and 3D content under multimodal control. However, arbitrary user-defined modality-to-4D (X-to-4D) generation remains challenging due to the high cost of constructing diverse datasets and the limited scalability of existing methods. This paper presents Align4D, a flexible framework that translates any-modal input into coherent video-3D pairs, using video to guide 4D motion and 3D data to shape 4D geometry. Align4D introduces three key techniques: (1) Object Distance Alignment, which searches Video-Aligned and Multiview-Aligned Object Distances (VAOD/MAOD), respectively, to reconcile 4D renderings with video and the priors of multiview diffusion models; (2) Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment, which constrains known and unknown views through synchronized video and 3D inputs, ensuring consistent 4D generation; and (3) Asynchronous Optimization, which decouples Gaussian attribute and deformation network training to enhance motion and geometry fidelity. We further propose the X4D dataset, which integrates prompt, image, video, and 3D data for benchmarking. Experiments on X4D and Consistent4D demonstrate that Align4D achieves state-of-the-art quality and consistency in X-to-4D generation. Project page: https://miaoqiaowei.github.io/Align4D/.

</details>


### [127] [WorldDirector: Building Controllable World Simulators with Persistent Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02517)
*Hanlin Wang,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Wen Wang,Qingyan Bai,Ka Leong Cheng,Yue Yu,Yixuan Li,Yihao Meng,Zichen Liu,Yanhong Zeng,Yujun Shen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldDirector提出了一种高度可控的视频世界模型框架，通过LLM编排3D轨迹与相机运动，将语义运动编排与视觉生成解耦，实现了动态物体的持久记忆和视角自由探索，即使物体长时间离开视野后重新进入，也能保持其精确视觉身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型将物理动力学与像素渲染纠缠在一起，依赖连续视觉观察来维持运动，导致动态物体离开视野后身份丢失，无法实现持久化的动态物体记忆和自由视角探索。

Method: 利用LLM协调3D轨迹与相机运动，将编排好的轨迹作为控制信号输入视频生成模型，显式解耦语义运动编排与视觉生成，保证物理逻辑和外观稳定性。

Result: 支持合成复杂和扩展事件，具有前所未有的可控性和持久动态物体记忆能力，物体长时间离开视野后重新进入仍能保持精确视觉身份。

Conclusion: WorldDirector通过解耦运动编排与视觉生成，成功实现了可控视频世界模型中动态物体的持久记忆和自由视角探索，为复杂长事件合成提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present WorldDirector, a highly controllable video world model framework designed for persistent dynamic object memory and unrestricted viewpoint exploration. Unlike existing world models that entangle physical dynamics with pixel rendering and rely on continuous visual observation to sustain motion, our framework explicitly decouples semantic motion orchestration from visual generation. By leveraging an LLM to coordinate 3D trajectories with camera movements and subsequently employing these orchestrated trajectories as control signals for video generation, our approach ensures strict physical logic and appearance stability, successfully preserving the exact visual identities of dynamic entities even when they re-enter the scene after prolonged periods out of view. Experimental results demonstrate that our method supports the synthesis of complex and extended events with unprecedented controllability and persistent dynamic object memory. Project Page: https://worlddirector.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [WaveLander: A Generalizable Hierarchical Control Framework for UAV Landing on Wave-Disturbed Platforms via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01281)
*Chun-Kit Li,Iok Long Sit,Ming Fung Siu,Ka Yu Kui,Hin Wang Lin,Pengyu Wang,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WaveLander提出了一种分层强化学习控制框架，将无人机在波浪扰动海洋平台上的自主着陆解耦为高层RL策略（输出垂直速度参考）和底层传统飞行控制器（负责姿态稳定与横向跟踪），在仿真中展现了鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在波浪扰动平台上着陆的难题：平台随机运动、时变姿态和不确定触地条件使现有模型方法依赖精确预测和在线优化，而端到端学习训练复杂度高且可解释性差。

Method: 分层控制框架：RL策略将平台相对观测映射为标量垂直速度参考，底层传统控制器维持姿态稳定和横向跟踪；将动态平台着陆降维成低维时序感知控制问题。

Result: 在随机波浪扰动仿真中，WaveLander实现了鲁棒着陆性能，并能泛化到未见过的扰动条件。

Conclusion: 分层学习控制方法在舰载无人机回收任务中具有潜力，有效简化了复杂动态环境下的着陆决策问题。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) on wave-disturbed marine platforms remains challenging due to stochastic platform motion, time-varying platform attitude, and uncertain touchdown conditions. Existing model-based methods often require accurate motion prediction and online optimization, while end-to-end learning approaches may suffer from high training complexity and limited interpretability. This paper presents WaveLander, a hierarchical control framework via reinforcement learning (RL) that decouples vertical landing decision-making from low-level flight stabilization. The RL policy maps a compact platform-relative observation to a scalar vertical velocity reference, while a conventional low-level flight controller maintains attitude stability and lateral tracking. This formulation reduces dynamic platform landing to a low-dimensional, timing-aware control problem and enables smooth landing behavior without explicit switching rules. Simulation results under randomized wave-induced platform motions show that WaveLander achieves robust landing performance and generalizes to unseen disturbance conditions, demonstrating the potential of hierarchical learning-based control for marine UAV recovery.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive Companionship for Group-Following Robots: Handling Dynamically Changing Group Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01287)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的社交机器人自适应群体伴随方法，通过VLM语义推理推断伴随位置、维持社交距离、理解群体动态，并结合MPPI控制器保证稳定性，在五个场景中成功率和碰撞率分别提升15%和降低25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类群体通常不遵循固定队形，使得机器人在保持自然的群体伴随行为方面面临重大挑战，现有方法难以适应动态变化的群体结构。

Method: 先检测群体成员，感知模块生成交互群体空间的视觉表征作为VLM输入，VLM进行语义推理确定伴随位置和社交距离，再结合Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI)控制器实现稳定安全控制。

Result: 在五个实验场景中，成功率提升15%，碰撞率降低25%；用户研究表明机器人产生的伴随行为被视为自然且社交恰当的。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的语义推理能力可以有效提升机器人在非固定队形群体中的自适应伴随能力，生成更自然的社交行为。

Abstract: Accompanying a group of humans is an essential aspect of developing human-like social cognition in robots. However, human groups typically do not follow fixed formations, which poses significant challenges for robots in maintaining natural companionship behaviors. In this paper, we propose an adaptive group-accompaniment method for social robots based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), leveraging their semantic reasoning capabilities to infer companion positions, maintain social distances, and understand group dynamics. The members of the group are first detected, and a perceptual module generates visual representations of the interaction group space as input to the VLM, which is then combined with a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller to ensure stability and safety. Experimental evaluations across five scenarios show that the proposed method enables robots to accompany the group effectively, demonstrating a 15\% improvement in success rate and a 25\% reduction in collision rate compared to baseline approaches. Additionally, a user study indicates that the generated companionship behaviors are perceived as natural and socially appropriate.

</details>


### [130] [The Three Dimensions of ROS 2 Middleware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01304)
*Sanghoon Lee,Taehun Kim,Angelo Corsaro,Kyung-Joon Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统综述ROS 2中间件架构，从Space（空间抽象）、Time（时间可预测性）、State（状态连续性）三个结构维度分析其在动态无线环境下的局限性与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROS 2中间件（DDS、Zenoh）在资源受限无线环境下存在结构性局限，缺乏系统性归因分析。本文旨在建立统一框架揭示这些局限的根源。

Method: 提出Space-Time-State三维分析框架，对现有中间件实现的发现机制、数据交换、状态管理进行结构化综述，归纳各维度间的权衡关系。

Result: 空间抽象会模糊网络可变性、削弱时间保证；状态连续性引入的计算/网络开销与实时通信冲突。揭示了现代机器人中间件的结构性权衡与实用极限。

Conclusion: 指出现有建模与分析方法的缺口，提出鲁棒可扩展机器人中间件架构的研究路线图。

Abstract: ROS 2 (Robot Operating System 2) has emerged as the de facto standard for modern robot software development, with middleware implementations such as the Data Distribution Service (DDS) and Zenoh forming the core infrastructure for distributed robotic communication. Despite their architectural flexibility, these middleware systems exhibit structural limitations, particularly under dynamic and resource-constrained wireless environments. This paper presents a systematic survey of ROS 2 middleware and introduces a conceptual framework to examine its architectural limits through three structural dimensions required by distributed robotic systems, namely Space, Time, and State. We first provide a structured analysis of middleware architecture and operational dynamics, including discovery, data exchange, and state management mechanisms. Building on this foundation, we formalize Time as temporal predictability for control loops, Space as spatial abstraction from physical topology to enable modular deployment, and State as contextual continuity despite dynamic node participation and intermittent connectivity. Through a comprehensive review of existing implementations and prior studies, we organize middleware research according to the structural trade-offs that arise among these dimensions. Under constrained wireless conditions, spatial abstraction can obscure network variability and weaken temporal guarantees, while mechanisms that preserve state continuity introduce computational and network overhead that competes with time-critical communication. These interactions reveal structural trade-offs that characterize the practical limits of contemporary robot middleware. By synthesizing architectural patterns and identifying gaps in current modeling and analysis approaches, this survey outlines a principled research roadmap for robust and scalable robotic middleware architectures.

</details>


### [131] [Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guidance for Vision-Language-Action Models via Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01378)
*William English,Hao Zheng,Rickard Ewetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号安全引导机制，在流匹配VLA模型的去噪过程中通过最小范数约束优化修正轨迹预测，实现预测性碰撞避免，在SafeLIBERO上相比单步方法任务成功率提升19.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的安全措施仅能防止下一步动作导致的碰撞，缺乏对长时域轨迹的预测性安全保障，尤其长期任务中分布偏移加剧了碰撞风险。

Method: 将安全约束表述为最小范数约束优化问题，在流匹配VLA的迭代去噪过程中修正中间噪声轨迹预测中的安全违规，实现符号约束与神经轨迹生成的交织。

Result: SafeLIBERO上碰撞避免82.8%，任务成功81.6%，相比单步方法分别提升6.3%和19.8%，长期任务上增益最大。

Conclusion: 神经符号安全引导机制能有效实现预测性碰撞避免，在长期horizon任务中优势显著，为VLA模型的安全部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising generalization capabilities across robotic manipulation tasks, yet their real-world deployment remains limited by the lack of effective safety measures. Specifically, existing safety measures only prevent collisions caused by the robot's next action. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic safety guidance mechanism for flow matching based VLAs that enables predictive collision avoidance. Flow matching based VLAs determine the next actions by predicting a trajectory (a sequence of actions) through an iterative neural flow matching process. Our method formulates safety enforcement as a minimum-norm constrained optimization problem that corrects safety violations during the denoising process of noisy intermediate trajectory predictions. By analyzing predicted trajectories and applying corrections during iterative denoising, our approach anticipates collisions before they become unavoidable. This interleaving of symbolic constraint satisfaction with neural trajectory generation enables predictive collision avoidance rather than reactive intervention. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, our method achieves 82.8% collision avoidance and 81.6% task success, a 6.3% and 19.8% improvement respectively over single-step methods, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks where compounding distribution shift is most pronounced. Video demonstrations of our approach are included on our project page at https://willenglish.tech/SafetyGuidedFlowMatching/.

</details>


### [132] [CommonRoad-Game: A Human-in-the-Loop Simulation Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01382)
*Yunfei Bi,Youran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CommonRoad-Game是一个轻量级人在环仿真框架，紧密集成CommonRoad平台，用于运动规划器的系统测试和人类驾驶行为分析，具备多线程架构、时间同步机制和场景生成模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台要么依赖记录数据集、缺乏实时人机交互接口，要么与自动驾驶生态系统集成弱，且多数人在环模拟器计算开销大，不适合快速原型开发和灵活实验。

Method: 提出多线程架构+鲁棒同步机制（仿真时间与墙钟时间对齐），实现自动驾驶与人类驾驶车辆间的确定性和时间一致性交互；提供场景生成模块记录驾驶日志，构建多样化可复现测试用例。

Result: 实现了稳定的时间同步，支持可扩展的多智能体仿真，无缝集成CommonRoad兼容的运动规划器生成交互式驾驶场景。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了轻量级人在环仿真在自动驾驶早期研究中的空白，开源且易于集成到现有生态系统中。

Abstract: Motion planning algorithms should be evaluated in human-in-the-loop environments to ensure they produce safe and efficient behaviors during interactions. However, existing simulation platforms often rely on recorded datasets, lack dedicated interfaces for real-time human interaction, or remain weakly integrated with an autonomous driving ecosystem. Moreover, many human-in-the-loop simulators are computationally intensive by design, making them less suitable for rapid prototyping and flexible experimentation in early-stage autonomous driving research. To address these limitations, we present CommonRoad-Game, a lightweight human-in-the-loop simulation framework tightly integrated with the CommonRoad platform, focusing on the systematic testing of motion planners with human participation and the analysis of human driving behaviors in interactive scenarios. We introduce a multi-threaded architecture with a robust synchronization mechanism that aligns simulation time with wall-clock time, enabling deterministic and temporally consistent interaction between autonomous and human-driven vehicles. In addition, the framework provides a scenario generation module that records driving logs, allowing diverse and reproducible test cases to be constructed from human-in-the-loop experiments. Experimental results demonstrate that CommonRoad-Game achieves stable temporal synchronization, supports scalable multi-agent simulation, and seamlessly integrates CommonRoad-compatible motion planners to generate interactive driving scenarios. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Yunfei-Bi8/CommonRoad-Game.

</details>


### [133] [BIFROST: Bridging Invariant Feature Representation for Observation-space Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01410)
*Yunfu Deng,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BIFROST方法，通过跨域双模拟目标学习共享历史编码器，将跨域中产生等价长期行为的观察-动作序列映射到相近的隐状态，实现策略从仿真到现实的零样本迁移，在视觉导航和接触丰富操作任务中优于域适应和协同训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sim2real方法通常针对渲染或物理等单个领域差距分别设计适应模块，当多种差距共存时组合堆叠，但忽视了仿真与现实之间存在共享结构——等价动作从等价配置出发会产生等价长期结果。本文旨在从原始观测中识别并利用这种共享结构，实现零样本迁移。

Method: 提出BIFROST，核心是跨域双模拟目标：对配对的跨域数据学习共享历史编码器，使得在不同领域（仿真/现实）中产生等价长期行为的观测-动作序列被映射到相近的隐状态，在此隐状态上训练策略后从仿真零样本迁移到现实。

Result: 在sim2sim视觉导航、sim2real接触丰富操作任务和视觉伺服任务上，BIFROST实现了有效的零样本迁移，而域适应和协同训练基线在视觉和动力学领域差距并存时均失败。

Conclusion: 跨域双模拟方法能够有效捕捉不同领域间的共享结构，在无需额外域适应模块的情况下实现从仿真到现实的零样本策略迁移，为sim2real问题提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Sim2real transfer for robot policy learning suffers due to mismatch between simulation and reality. Existing methods typically address each gap in isolation through separate adaptation modules, which are composed or layered when both gaps coexist. Yet the basis for attempting sim2real in the first place is that there is shared structure between a task in simulation and reality, where equivalent actions from equivalent configurations produce equivalent long term outcomes regardless of domain specific differences in rendering or physics. In this paper, we study whether we can identify and exploit this shared structure from raw observations to train a policy that enables zero shot transfer. We introduce BIFROST, which learns a shared history encoder on paired cross-domain data via cross-domain bisimulation objective: observation-action sequences leading to equivalent long-term behavior are mapped to nearby latent states, regardless of domain. Policies trained on these latent states in simulation transfer zero-shot to reality. We provide empirical evidence on sim2sim visual navigation and sim2real contact rich manipulation task and visual servoing task that BIFROST achieves effective transfer where domain adaptation and co-training baselines fail under both visual and dynamics domain gaps.

</details>


### [134] [SE(2) Navigation Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01454)
*Shuyang Shi,Kaixian Qu,Changan Chen,Ines Kast,Yuntao Ma,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SE(2)导航网格，在传统NavMesh基础上编码偏航相关的可通行性，并配套ASA分层路径规划策略，支持在线更新，在仿真和实体机器人上验证了狭窄空间中的导航性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航网格（NavMesh）假设yaw不变的可通行性，无法用于非圆形机器人在受限空间中的导航；现有的点云/体素/网格表示在可通行性估计或路径规划效率上各有不足。

Method: 构建多yaw层的多边形可通行区域图，通过足迹掩码评估各朝向的可通行性；提出A*-String Pulling-A* (ASA)三级分层规划优化位置和朝向；提出从流式点云增量更新SE(2) NavMesh的在线方法。

Result: 仿真中相比经典NavMesh多捕获50%+可通行区域；ASA在受限环境中一致优于基于采样的基线；真实机器人验证了在线生成和多环境导航成功。

Conclusion: SE(2) NavMesh解决了非圆形机器人在复杂多层环境中的导航问题，兼顾表示精度与规划效率，且支持在线更新。

Abstract: Global navigation for ground robots in complex multi-level environments requires representations that accurately capture traversable regions while enabling efficient path planning. Current approaches present key limitations: Point clouds and volumetric occupancy maps lack explicit surface structure for traversability estimation, whereas direct pathfinding on dense triangle meshes is computationally prohibitive. Navigation meshes mitigate these challenges through polygonal abstraction of the underlying mesh, but assume yaw-invariant traversability, rendering them unsuitable for non-circular robots in constrained spaces. We propose SE(2) Navigation Mesh (SE(2) NavMesh), a polygonal representation of traversable regions that encodes yaw-dependent traversability. Our method evaluates traversability using footprint masks and constructs a graph over yaw-specific layers with explicit translational and rotational connectivity. Grounded in this representation, we develop an A*-String Pulling-A* (ASA) pathfinding strategy that hierarchically optimizes robot position and heading. We also present an online method that incrementally updates the SE(2) NavMesh from streaming point clouds during concurrent geometry reconstruction. In simulation, the SE(2) NavMesh captures over 50% more traversable area than classical NavMeshes, and the SE(2) NavMesh + ASA pipeline consistently outperforms sampling-based baselines in constrained environments. Extensive real-world experiments on a physical robot validate real-time online generation and successful navigation across multiple environments.

</details>


### [135] [A Reconfigurable Rocker-Bogie Robot for High Step Climbing and Turning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01554)
*Kento Koizumi,Tomoaki Ohba,Yuta Saito,Takeya Morito,Kenji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可重构摇杆-转向架机构，通过主动摆动转向架实现四/六轮构型切换，并在四轮构型下利用差速驱动全向轮实现零半径转向，同时保持高越障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摇杆-转向架机构在移动机器人的转向灵活性与越障能力之间存在矛盾：六轮非转向构型越障强但转向半径大、速度慢；四轮构型转向灵活但越障能力有限。本文旨在通过可重构设计同时实现高效转向和高越障性能。

Method: 在转向架关节处安装电机，主动控制转向架上下摆动来实现四/六轮构型切换；在摇杆末端安装全向轮，四轮构型下基于差速驱动模型实现转向。搭建原型机器人并进行实验验证。

Result: 零半径转向速度是传统六轮非转向机构的5倍以上，总平均轮扭矩仅为传统机构的约17%；成功攀爬40 cm台阶，平均攀爬时间6.4秒。

Conclusion: 所提机构在保证高越障能力的同时大幅提升了转向效率和速度，验证了可重构设计在移动机器人领域的有效性。

Abstract: This study proposes a reconfigurable rocker-bogie mechanism that achieves efficient turning motion with a small number of actuators while maintaining high step-climbing capability. By installing motors at the bogie joints and actively swinging up and down bogies, the system enables switching between four-wheel and six-wheel configurations. Omnidirectional wheels are mounted on the rear ends of the rockers, allowing smooth turning in the four-wheel configuration based on a differential-drive model. Experimental evaluation using a prototype robot demonstrated that the proposed mechanism achieves zero-radius turning at a speed more than five times that of a conventional rocker-bogie mechanism equipped with six non-steerable grip wheels, while requiring only approximately 17% of the total average wheel torque. In addition, the robot successfully climbed a 40 cm step with an average climbing time of 6.4 s, confirming its high turning and step-climbing performance.

</details>


### [136] [Multi-Rate Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Wall-Supported Bipedal Locomotion of Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01574)
*Taizoon Chunawala,Jeeseop Kim,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多速率非线性模型预测控制的分层规划与控制框架，使四足机器人能在受限环境中借助墙壁支撑实现混合双足运动，高层MR-NMPC动态规划接触点与质心/姿态轨迹，低层全身控制器跟踪参考，在Unitree A1仿真中成功率达到传统MPC的2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在受限环境中执行混合双足运动时，控制器需要同时规划接触点和连续轨迹，面临非线性动力学、单边接触约束、欠驱动和动力学切换等挑战，现有启发式足部放置策略动态稳定性不足。

Method: 高层采用MR-NMPC基于单刚体动力学模型同时规划离散接触点轨迹和连续质心/姿态轨迹；低层采用基于虚拟约束和二次规划的非线性全身控制器跟踪参考并执行全阶动力学。

Result: 在Unitree A1四足机器人仿真中验证了在粗糙地形和外力扰动下的鲁棒壁辅助双足运动；MR-NMPC在不规则地形高速运动中的成功率是传统MPC加启发式足部放置的2.9倍。

Conclusion: 将接触点规划纳入多速率最优控制框架可提升动态稳定性，该方法能有效应对受限环境中的复杂双足运动任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel layered planning and control framework based on multi-rate nonlinear model predictive control (MR-NMPC) that enables quadrupedal robots to perform hybrid bipedal locomotion with wall-assisted support in constrained environments. Real-time trajectory optimization for this locomotion presents significant challenges, as the controller must simultaneously plan for both the contact points and the continuous trajectories of the robot's center of mass (CoM) and orientation within the robot's nonlinear dynamics while accounting for unilateral contact constraints, underactuation, and the switching nature of the robot's dynamics. At the high level of the control framework, an MR-NMPC is proposed, which dynamically plans both the discrete-time trajectories of the contact points and the continuous-time trajectories of the CoM and orientation, using a single rigid body (SRB) dynamics model. By incorporating contact-point planning within the multi-rate optimal control framework, this approach enhances dynamic stability compared to heuristic foot placement strategies. At the low level of the control framework, a nonlinear whole-body controller (WBC) based on virtual constraints and a quadratic program enforces full-order dynamics and tracks the MR-NMPC references. The proposed approach is validated through extensive numerical simulations demonstrating the robust wall-assisted bipedal locomotion of a Unitree A1 quadrupedal robot on rough terrains and under external disturbances in a constrained environment. Comparative analysis shows that the proposed MR-NMPC achieves a 2.9 times higher success rate compared to conventional MPC with heuristic-based foot placement strategies in negotiating irregular terrain at high speeds.

</details>


### [137] [Path planning for unmanned naval surface vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01631)
*Daniel G. Schwartz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合全局与局部路径规划器的USV避障新方法。全局规划器融合Grassfire、改进版Grassfire和新版概率路线图三种算法；局部规划器基于障碍物相对全局路径的运动方向，决策从障碍物后方绕行的避障策略。仿真验证并对比了D*算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有USV避障方法在处理固定与移动障碍物时仍存在挑战，尤其是在移动障碍物避让策略上缺乏系统性决策逻辑，现有方法多与障碍物并行而非规划从后方绕过的最优路径。

Method: 全局规划器：组合Grassfire、改进Grassfire和新版PRM三种路径规划算法。局部规划器：基于障碍物相对USV全局路径的运动方向，通过高层决策逻辑确定最优避障策略，系统地将USV导向障碍物后方而不是并行等待。

Result: 仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性，并与D*算法及其他动态路径规划系统进行了对比。

Conclusion: 提出的全局-局部混合规划框架能有效处理固定和移动障碍物的实时避障问题，局部规划器从障碍物后方绕行的策略优于传统的并行等待策略。

Abstract: There nowadays is a myriad of approaches to real-time avoidance of fixed obstacles for unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) and, to a lesser extent, also the task of avoiding moving obstacles such as boats, ships, swimmers, and other USVs, but both topics still present challenges. This paper offers novel approaches to both of these problems. It uses a combination of a global path planner, which finds a path from a start point to a goal point that avoids fixed obstacles (given that their locations are known in advance), and a local path planner, which can circumnavigate a moving obstacle (as well as any previously unknown fixed obstacles). The global planner is novel in that it employs a combination of three path planners, one known in the literature as Grassfire, one that is a new modification of Grassfire, and one that is a new, and arguably more intuitive, version of the well-known Probabilistic Roadmap. The local planner is novel in that it employs a higher-level decision logic based on its observations regarding the direction of movement of the obstacle relative to the USVs global path. This logic enables the USV to determine the best strategy for avoiding the obstacle by systematically routing the vehicle behind the obstacle rather than running parallel to it until the opportunity to pass appears. Simulations are provided that validate these claims. For comparison with other systems, the simulations include an implementation of the well-known D* algorithm, and the discussion covers additional dynamic path planning systems, which, like D*, do not necessarily route the vehicle behind the moving obstacle.

</details>


### [138] [One Demonstration Is Enough for Real-World Robotic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01651)
*Yuwan Liu,Hongze Yu,Song Liu,Yuhan Wang,Junge Zhang,Yaodong Yang,Yuanpei Chen,Ceyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoSERL通过单次人类演示，利用滑动窗口干预、安全恢复和自动终止三种机制，实现了完全自动化的真实世界机器人强化学习，在6个接触密集型操作任务中超越了需要20次演示的SERL等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人RL面临数据收集成本高和奖励设计困难的问题；现有方法要么需要大量演示，要么依赖训练过程中持续的人类干预，缺乏利用单次演示实现完全自动化干预的框架。

Method: 提出三种互补机制：(1) 滑动窗口干预机制，持续引导探索防止局部最优和安全偏离；(2) 安全恢复机制，通过预定义轨迹恢复点检测和纠正失败状态；(3) 干预终止准则，当策略能独立完成任务时自动关闭引导，保留探索优势。

Result: 在两个机器人平台六项接触密集型操作任务上，AutoSERL一致优于使用20次演示初始化的SERL、行为克隆和专用单次模仿学习基线MILES，达到与HIL-SERL（人类循环干预）相当的性能，在插入任务上实现100%成功率，且对位置变化具有更好鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种仅需单次演示即可实现真实世界机器人RL全自动化干预的实用框架，显著降低了对演示数量和人机交互的需求。

Abstract: Learning effective robot control policies on physical hardware is challenging due to costly data collection and the difficulty of reward specification. Prior work has incorporated demonstrations into reinforcement learning (RL), yet existing approaches either require large numbers of demonstrations or depend on continuous human intervention during training. To address these limitations, we present AutoSERL, a framework that leverages a single demonstration to fully automate the intervention process in real-world robot RL. The framework includes three complementary mechanisms to accomplish certain tasks: a sliding window intervention mechanism that continuously guides exploration to prevent local optima and unsafe deviations, a safety recovery mechanism that detects and corrects failure states via predefined trajectory recovery points, and an intervention termination criterion that automatically disables guidance once the policy can independently complete the task, preserving its exploration advantage. We evaluate AutoSERL on six contact-intensive manipulation tasks across two robot platforms, spanning insertion, hanging, and hinge-based tasks. AutoSERL consistently outperforms SERL initialized with 20 demonstrations, behavior cloning, and MILES -- a dedicated one-shot imitation learning baseline -- across all tasks while matching HIL-SERL, achieves 100% success rate on insertion tasks, and demonstrates improved robustness to positional variations, all from a single demonstration. Code and videos are available on our project website: https://autoserl.github.io/.

</details>


### [139] [Imagining the Sense of Touch: Touch-Informed Manipulation via Imagined Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01684)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Jyun-Chi Hu,Yosuke Saka,Quan Khanh Luu,Jiuzhou Lei,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Bihao Zhang,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacImag是一种触觉想象框架，通过视觉和本体感受预测触觉观测信号，在不使用触觉传感器的情况下指导操作策略，在模拟和真实任务中均显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知能提升机器人操作性能，但触觉硬件脆弱、校准和维护成本高，限制了实际部署。核心问题是：机器人能否在不部署触觉传感器的情况下获得触觉知识的益处？

Method: 提出TacImag框架，从配对视觉-触觉示范中学习，在测试时仅用视觉观测预测触觉信号（力场/触觉图像），并用生成的触觉信号引导操作策略。

Result: 在6个模拟和4个真实操作任务上验证：真实实验中触觉力场提升接触敏感任务44.4%，触觉图像提升纹理敏感任务23.3%。

Conclusion: 触觉想象并非简单恢复缺失的触觉测量，而是一种接触感知的监督形式，将细微视觉交互线索转化为策略更易利用的表征。触觉表示与任务需求的匹配度决定想象效果。

Abstract: Tactile sensing can substantially improve contact-rich robotic manipulation, yet its practical deployment remains limited by the fragility, calibration requirements, and maintenance burden of tactile hardware. This raises a fundamental question: can robots benefit from tactile knowledge without requiring tactile sensors at deployment? We present TacImag, a tactile imagination framework that predicts tactile observations from vision and proprioception and uses the generated signals to guide manipulation policies. Trained from paired visuotactile demonstrations, TacImag enables touch-informed manipulation using only visual observations at test time. We evaluate TacImag on six simulated and four real-world manipulation tasks. Across simulation and real-world experiments, imagined tactile observations consistently improve manipulation performance without requiring tactile hardware. In real-world experiments, imagined force fields improve contact-sensitive tasks by 44.4% on average, whereas imagined tactile images improve texture-sensitive tasks by 23.3%, revealing that the effectiveness of tactile imagination depends strongly on the relationship between tactile representation and task requirements. Our results further suggest that tactile imagination does not simply recover missing tactile measurements. Instead, it acts as a form of contact-aware supervision that transforms subtle visual interaction cues into representations that are easier for manipulation policies to exploit.

</details>


### [140] [CoRe: Combined Rewards with Vision-Language Model Feedback for Preference-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01721)
*Hexian Ni,Tao Lu,Yinghao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoRe混合框架，将奖励分解为正式奖励(FR)和残差奖励(RR)，利用视觉语言模型自动构建偏好对齐的奖励，无需人工参与，在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操作任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计奖励函数困难且可能导致次优策略，而从偏好中学习的奖励存在效率低和训练不稳定问题。受认知科学中人类学习的双重性启发，希望将两者互补结合。

Method: (1) Formal Reward Module (FRM): 利用VLM基于任务知识和偏好反馈迭代设计优化正式奖励；(2) Residual Reward Module (RRM): 利用VLM生成视频级偏好标签，学习残差奖励以捕捉隐含偏好。两个模块协同工作。

Result: 在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操作任务上，策略学习效果和效率均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CoRe实现了无需人工参与的、偏好对齐的、高效可靠的奖励自动构建，在机器人操作任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Hand-crafted rewards are often difficult to specify and may lead to suboptimal policies, while learned rewards from preferences can suffer from inefficiency and unstable training. Inspired by the dual nature of human learning explored in cognitive science, we decompose rewards into two complementary components: Formal Rewards (FR), explicitly designed based on task knowledge, and Residual Rewards (RR), learned from observations to capture implicit and nuanced preferences. Based on this decomposition, we propose CoRe, a hybrid framework that integrates FR and RR with vision-language models (VLMs) feedback to achieve preference-aligned policies without human involvement. Our contributions are twofold: (1) We propose a Formal Reward Module (FRM) that leverages VLMs to iteratively design and optimize FR based on task knowledge and preference feedback, enabling the continual improvement of policy during training; (2) We introduce a Residual Reward Module (RRM) that learns RR from video-level preference by employing VLMs to generate preference labels and capturing nuanced rewards that complement FR, ensuring alignment with human intent. Through the synergy of FRM and RRM, CoRe enables the automatic construction of reliable rewards that are efficient and preference-aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoRe outperforms existing approaches in terms of policy learning effectiveness and efficiency on ten robotic manipulation tasks in simulation and five real-worlds. Videos can be found on our project website: https://core-2026.github.io/

</details>


### [141] [Lightweight Safe Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01794)
*Shenghui Zhang,YuXuan Gao,Songwei Zhao,Jifeng Hu,Zijing Zhang,Hechang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种安全约束的感知-控制集成框架，使用轻量级网络编码稀疏观测为碰撞风险感知特征，结合基于拉格朗日的安全PPO算法和分层控制架构，在密集环境中实现UAV自主导航，在变密度障碍物和不同飞行速度下优于现有RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法缺乏显式安全机制，不安全探索和冒险行为在高速飞行中尤为严重；安全RL方法通过投影策略输出到安全动作集可能引入不稳定；学习型方法依赖密集输入或大网络，难以轻量化部署。

Method: 轻量级网络（非对称+深度可分离卷积）编码稀疏观测为碰撞风险感知特征；将任务建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），采用分层控制架构，用基于拉格朗日的安全PPO算法求解；结合课程学习提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在变密度障碍物和不同飞行速度实验中，成功率、安全性和效率均优于现有RL基线。

Conclusion: 提出的安全约束感知-控制集成框架有效提升了UAV在密集环境中的自主导航安全性和效率，轻量级设计适合机载部署。

Abstract: With the rapid development of autonomous aerial systems, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed in applications such as inspection, environmental monitoring, and rescue, creating growing demand for reliable autonomous navigation. However, autonomous UAV navigation in dense environments remains challenging under sparse perception and dynamic constraints. Most reinforcement learning (RL) methods lack explicit safety mechanisms, leading to unsafe exploration, unstable training, and risky behaviors, especially during high-speed flight. Even in safe RL approaches, safety is often enforced by projecting policy outputs onto a safe action set, which may introduce instability. Meanwhile, many learning-based methods rely on dense inputs or large networks, increasing computational burden and limiting lightweight onboard deployment. Facing the above challenges, we propose a safety-constrained perception-control integrated framework for UAV navigation. A lightweight network encodes sparse observations into collision-risk-aware features using asymmetric and depthwise separable convolutions. We formulate the task as a constrained Markov decision process within a hierarchical control architecture and solve it using a Lagrangian-based safe PPO algorithm. Curriculum learning further improves training stability. Experiments with varying obstacle densities and flight speeds demonstrate higher success rates, improved safety, and better efficiency than existing reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [142] [VLA-Corrector: Lightweight Detect-and-Correct Inference for Adaptive Action Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01804)
*Yi Pan,Miao Pan,Qi Lu,Jiaming Huang,Man Zhang,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Jie Zhang,Yongliang Shen,Xuhong Zhang,Wenqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA-Corrector，一种轻量级矫正推理框架，通过在动作分块VLA策略中引入潜空间视觉监控器（LVM）和在线梯度引导（OGG）的检测-纠正机制，实现事件触发的自适应动作时域，在不重新训练骨干网络的前提下提升接触丰富机器人操作的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略采用固定动作分块（action chunk）的"预测-盲目执行"开环模式，在接触丰富的物理交互中，微小局部扰动会快速累积放大的盲区内导致复合误差和任务失败，缺乏闭环响应能力。

Method: VLA-Corrector包含两个核心组件：1) Latent-space Vision Monitor (LVM)：持续比较预测与实际的视觉特征演化，在线检测视觉动态偏差；2) Online Gradient Guidance (OGG)：检测到持续偏差后触发截断事件，丢弃剩余过时动作并调用矫正重规划。由此自然形成事件触发的自适应动作时域。

Result: 在不修改骨干网络权重、不重新训练VLA骨干的前提下，在长时域、接触丰富的机器人操作任务中显著提升鲁棒性，保持动作分块的大部分效率优势，打断复合误差累积。

Conclusion: VLA-Corrector有效缓解了静态动作时域在执行鲁棒性与策略调用频率之间的权衡，是一种轻量级、可即插即用的矫正推理框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently achieved strong progress in embodied intelligence. To reduce policy-call frequency while preserving temporal coherence, most generative policies adopt an action chunk mechanism, executing multiple future actions in an open-loop manner under a fixed action horizon. However, this "predict-then-blindly-execute" paradigm sacrifices closed-loop reactivity: in contact-rich physical interactions, even small local perturbations can rapidly amplify within the open-loop blind spot, leading to compounding errors and ultimately task failure. To address this limitation, we propose VLA-Corrector, a lightweight corrective inference framework for action-chunked VLA policies. Without modifying the backbone policy weights, VLA-Corrector introduces a lightweight Latent-space Vision Monitor (LVM) that continuously compares predicted and actual visual feature evolution, enabling online detection of visual dynamics deviations. Once persistent deviation is detected, the system triggers a truncation event, discards the remaining stale actions, and invokes corrective replanning via Online Gradient Guidance (OGG). The detect-and-correct mechanism of VLA-Corrector naturally induces an event-triggered adaptive action horizon: it preserves long-horizon execution when the current chunk remains reliable, and invokes short-horizon corrective replanning when execution begins to drift. In doing so, VLA-Corrector mitigates the trade-off imposed by static horizons between execution robustness and policy-call frequency. It can be integrated into different VLA models without further retraining the VLA backbone, interrupting compounding errors while preserving much of the efficiency benefit of action chunking and substantially improving robustness in long-horizon, contact-rich robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [143] [DL-SLAM: Enabling High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting SLAM in Dynamic Environments based on Dual-Level Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01860)
*Ziheng Xu,Qingfeng Li,Xuefeng Liu,Chen Chen,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DL-SLAM提出了一种双层级概率框架，结合语义和几何信息计算动态概率图，在像素级和物体级两个层级上建模动态概率，从而在动态SLAM中实现无伪影的静态地图构建，同时利用瞬态静态物体提升位姿估计精度，在跟踪精度上提升高达13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态SLAM方法要么直接丢弃预定义的动态物体，忽略了瞬态静态物体可提供有价值的几何约束；要么使用逐像素不确定性图来量化运动幅度，但错误地将这些物体集成到静态地图中造成持久伪影，且纯几何信息导致不确定性图中物体边界模糊。

Method: 提出双层级概率框架：1) 结合语义和几何信息计算像素级动态概率图；2) 将像素级概率提升到3D并聚合得到每个实例的物体级动态概率；3) 利用物体级概率对动态高斯进行类别式修剪，生成无伪影静态地图；4) 静态地图提供几何一致的指导反向精化像素级概率。

Result: DL-SLAM在跟踪精度上比现有方法提升高达13%，同时生成高保真语义地图。

Conclusion: 双层级概率建模能有效区分动态和静态元素，既利用瞬态静态物体增强位姿估计，又避免伪影污染静态地图，且在精度和地图质量上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled significant progress in dense dynamic Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). Prevailing methods typically discard predefined dynamic objects, ignoring that transiently static objects offer valuable geometric constraints for pose estimation. A recent work attempts to leverage this potential by employing per-pixel uncertainty maps to quantify the magnitude of motion. While this approach enables transiently static objects to enhance pose estimation, it erroneously integrates these objects into the static map, resulting in persistent artifacts. Moreover, its reliance on purely geometric information leads to ambiguous object boundaries in the uncertainty maps. To overcome these limitations, we present DL-SLAM, a monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM system built upon a novel dual-level probabilistic framework. Our method computes dynamic probability maps by combining semantic and geometric information. These pixel-level probabilities are lifted to 3D and aggregated to derive an object-level dynamic probability for each instance. Object-level probability enables the categorical pruning of dynamic Gaussians, resulting in an artifact-free static map. The static map, in turn, provides a geometrically consistent guidance to refine the pixel-wise probabilities, enhancing their reliability. Experimental results demonstrate that DL-SLAM outperforms existing approaches, improving tracking accuracy by up to 13\% while generating high-fidelity semantic maps.

</details>


### [144] [SPLC: Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01925)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Fu,Haiwen Hu,Shiquan Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SPLC算法，通过社会偏好反馈机制自动生成偏好数据，消除离线强化学习在人群机器人导航中对手动奖励函数设计的依赖，并在仿真和真实TurtleBot4实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
